    ✔ free text, unknown names, a service or clinician field, bad links and condition-revealing location names never reach a notification (TC-NT-06) (25.18234ms)
    ✔ dedupe keys prevent double sends: a repeat creates nothing and queues nothing, also under concurrency (85.479426ms)

    ✔ cancel by dedupe-key prefix cancels queued rows of that resource only; sent rows are final (86.671201ms)
    ✔ in-app notices: a patient lists only their own sent notices and marks them read once (21.301298ms)

    ✔ inserts: a patient only for themselves, queued only; admin realm only to principals; a worker needs its patients scope (13.455807ms)

  ▶ migration 0204: the receipt table
    ✔ anchors exist, the receipt table is empty, and the lag is the age of the oldest anchor (14.307434ms)
    ✔ no application role can UPDATE, DELETE or TRUNCATE a receipt; app_worker cannot UPDATE an anchor (175.588428ms)
    ✔ a receipt cannot name another organisation than its anchor, and PUBLIC and app_owner hold no right on the table (12.226771ms)
  ✔ migration 0204: the receipt table (203.028784ms)
  ▶ audit.archive_anchors
    ✔ archives every anchor once: key, version and SHA-256 are on the receipt; a second run puts nothing (5.983017ms)
    ✔ an object-store error leaves the anchor for the next run and the lag grows; the retry archives it (70.871827ms)
    ✔ 412 with an equal body inserts a receipt with the existing version (crash between put and insert) (7.611552ms)
    ✔ 412 with a different body publishes audit.anchor_mismatch (archive_differs) and inserts nothing (27.736913ms)
    ✔ the task emits AuditAnchorArchiveLagSeconds on every run, 0 when nothing waits (59.525448ms)
  ✔ audit.archive_anchors (172.389255ms)
  ▶ audit.verify_anchors: the archive is the authority
    ✔ untouched data verifies clean with the archive, and the archive was actually read (13.145034ms)
    ✔ tamper: an event row changed and the anchor chain rewritten consistently as owner -> db_differs_from_archive and an alert (104.319365ms)
    ✔ an anchor missing from the database but present in the archive is db_differs_from_archive, unless it is after the restore point (19.29854ms)
    ✔ a deleted receipt is not_archived once the anchor is older than the grace; a fresh anchor is not flagged under the default grace (65.311935ms)
    ✔ a receipt that names a version the archive does not list is not_archived (forged receipt) (12.173221ms)
    ✔ a later version of an archived key is itself a finding (archive_extra_version), judged on the earliest version (10.418879ms)
    ✔ an unreadable archive fails the task after the database checks and the mismatch events; it never reads as clean (17.337541ms)
  ✔ audit.verify_anchors: the archive is the authority (242.601875ms)

  ▶ the send job
    ✔ e-mail: renders the published version in the person's language, attaches the .ics, stores the vendor reference and no address or text (129.711689ms)
    ✔ language: the person's language when published, else the default; SMS and push render without a subject/with a title (148.378544ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-08: SMS off and e-mail on - the SMS is suppressed with its reason and the provider is never called; e-mail is sent (19.502454ms)
    ✔ suppression reasons: no address, unknown recipient, inactive patient, no contributor, no template (81.393188ms)
    ✔ in-app notices need no vendor and are sent even when every external channel is off (TC-NT-04) (6.037058ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-04: a failing provider is retried by the queue; the failure is an error CLASS; the last attempt publishes delivery_failed; recovery sends with the same token (101.212615ms)
    ✔ a vendor refusal (4xx) is final: failed, no queue retry, delivery_failed published (11.617618ms)
    ✔ quiet hours defer the send to when they end, restore the attempt count and queue the job (84.004339ms)
    ✔ a template that leaks text is stopped at send time by the output scan; the provider is never called (10.348326ms)
    ✔ a render error (a declared value missing) is a final failure, not a retry (8.246413ms)
    ✔ BE06-F1: a merge that re-points the row during the vendor call - sent, failed and deferred outcomes are still recorded (under the survivor) (101.167151ms)
    ✔ a patient merged while the job waited: the job follows the survivor and sends to the survivor's contacts (on_merged = resolve) (11.466213ms)
    ✔ a staff notice runs under the organisation scope and resolves through the principal contributor (69.879395ms)
    ✔ no address, message text or canary reaches a log line (0.259898ms)
  ✔ the send job (785.008345ms)
  ▶ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16)
    ✔ a receipt for a kept notification of a merged patient attaches to the SURVIVOR; a repeat of the event is a no-op (12.736848ms)
    ✔ untrusted payloads: unknown reference, bad shape, bad status, oversized and ambiguous references record nothing (7.836123ms)
    ✔ the inbound handler records through the same path (2.545928ms)
    ✔ the worker role cannot update or delete a receipt, or write one for a patient outside its job scope (3.429785ms)
